            SearchFlightController.changeDestination = $('#changeDestination').val();
            if(SearchFlightController.changeDestination) {
                $('.container-travel-details').css('display', 'block');
				SearchFlightController.showModalFlightSearchWarning();            	
			}
			SearchFlightController.setScrollAnimation();
        }
    });
</script>

		 * - na versao desktop, todas as datas sao objetos Date - cortesia do Datepicker;
		 * - na versao mobile, as datas sÃ£o obtidas de campos <input type="date"/>. Entao, precisam ser tratados de forma adequada.

	}, 2000);

<script>
	
		function urlParam(name) {
			var results = new RegExp('[\?&]' + name + '=([^&#]*)').exec(window.location.href);
			return results ? results[1] : null;  		 
		}
		
		// elemento principal da aba
		var uberAccrualTabElement = $(".uber-selection-accrual");
		var uberRedeemTabElement = $(".uber-selection-redeem");
		
		// radio button interno da aba
		var uberAccrualTabInputElement = $(".uber-selection-accrual input");
		var uberRedeemTabInputElement = $(".uber-selection-redeem input");
		
		
		// controle de seleção do input radio
		// foi feito por jQuery devido a conflitos de propriedades CSS
		uberAccrualTabElement.click(function() {
			uberAccrualTabInputElement.prop("checked", true);
			uberRedeemTabInputElement.prop("checked", false);
		});
		
		uberRedeemTabElement.click(function() {
			uberAccrualTabInputElement.prop("checked", false);
			uberRedeemTabInputElement.prop("checked", true);
		});
		
		// verifica se veio de redirecionamento de Resgate e muda aba se necessário
		var redeemUrlParam = urlParam('uber_redeem_value');
		
		if (redeemUrlParam) {
			uberRedeemTabElement.addClass('active');
			uberRedeemTabInputElement.prop("checked", true);
		} else {
			uberAccrualTabElement.addClass('active');
			uberAccrualTabInputElement.prop("checked", true);
		}

	    AUI().use(
	      'aui-tabview',
	      function(Y) {
	      
	        new Y.TabView({
	            srcNode: '#_118_INSTANCE_4ASXtTQdqETa_uberSelectionTabs'
	        }).render();
	        
	      }
	    );
	    
	</script>

<script>
	
		function urlParam(name) {
    		var results = new RegExp('[\?&]' + name + '=([^&#]*)').exec(window.location.href);
    		return results ? results[1] : null;  		 
		}

		var DEFAULT_TAB = "search-voos";
		var paramActiveTab = urlParam("active_tab") || DEFAULT_TAB;
		var tabs = ["search-voos", "search-hoteis", "search-carros", "search-uber", "search-ticket"];
		var isValidTab = (tabs.indexOf(paramActiveTab) > -1);

		if (!isValidTab) {
			var tab = $("." + DEFAULT_TAB);
		} else {
			var tab = $("." + paramActiveTab);
		}
		
		tab.addClass("active");	

	    AUI().use(

<script type="text/javascript">
    var t;
    function auto() {
        clearInterval(t)
        t = setTimeout(function () {
            $("#atomoKV .btn.right").click();
            auto();
        }, 6000)
    }

    function showBannerHomeItem(item, lista) {
        lista.find("li").fadeOut();
        lista.find("li[data-item='" + item + "']").fadeIn();
        clearInterval(t);
        auto();
    }

    function getNextBannerHomeItem(atual, listaItens) {
        var total = listaItens.length;
        if (atual + 1 > total) {
            return 1;
        } else {
            return atual + 1;
        }
    }

    function getPrevBannerHomeItem(atual, listaItens) {
        var total = listaItens.length;
        if (atual - 1 <= 0) {
            return total;
        } else {
            return atual - 1;
        }
    }

    function activeArowBannerHome(atual, listaItens, btns) {
        var total = listaItens.length;


        if (total <= 1) {
            btns.left.hide();
            btns.right.hide();
            return;
        }

        btns.left.fadeTo("fast", 1);
        btns.right.fadeTo("fast", 1);
        if (atual == total) {
            btns.right.fadeTo("fast", 0.3);
        }
        if (atual == 1) {
            btns.left.fadeTo("fast", 0.3);
        }
    }

    function setBannerHomeheight(height, banner) {
        banner.css("height", height + "px");
        banner.find("ul").css("height", height + "px");
        banner.find(".btn").css("height", height + "px");
    }

    $(document).ready(function () {
        var atual = 1;
        var touchstart = 0;
        var touchend = 0;
        var touchstartY = 0;
        var touchendY = 0;
        var clickTime = 100;
        var allowedTime = 1000; // tempo máximo para movimentar a distancia do swipe
        var threshold = 100; //distancia minima movimentada para considerar um swipe
        var dist;
        var elapsedTime;
        var startTime;
        var touchmoved;
        var banner = $("#atomoKV");
        var btns = {
            "left": banner.find('.left'),
            "right": banner.find('.right')
        }
        var lista = banner.find('ul');
        var listaItens = lista.find('li');

        listaItens.each(function (i) {
            $(this).attr("data-item", (i + 1));
            $(this).find(".atomoKVItem").addClass("atomoKVItem-" + (i + 1));
            if (atual == i + 1) {
                $(this).fadeIn(function () {
                    setBannerHomeheight(listaItens.find("img").height(), banner);
                });
            }
        })

        activeArowBannerHome(atual, listaItens, btns);

        btns.left.click(function () {
            var show = getPrevBannerHomeItem(atual, listaItens);
            activeArowBannerHome(show, listaItens, btns);
            showBannerHomeItem(show, lista);
            atual = show;
        });

        btns.right.click(function () {
            var show = getNextBannerHomeItem(atual, listaItens);
            activeArowBannerHome(show, listaItens, btns);
            showBannerHomeItem(show, lista);
            atual = show;
        });

        document.getElementById("atomoKV").addEventListener('touchstart', function (e) {            
            var touchobj = e.changedTouches[0]            
            dist = 0;
            touchstart = touchobj.pageX;
            touchstartY = touchobj.pageY;
            startTime = new Date().getTime() // guarda quando o usuário clica com o dedo na tela.
            console.log("touchstart: " + touchstart +"touchstartY: " + touchstartY + " Starttime: " + startTime);
           
        }, true);


        document.getElementById("atomoKV").addEventListener('touchend', function (e) {
            e.preventDefault();        
            var touchobj = e.changedTouches[0]
            touchend = touchobj.pageX;
            touchendY = touchobj.pageY;
            distY = Math.abs(touchendY - touchstartY);
            dist = Math.abs(touchend - touchstart); //guarda distancia total percorrida
            
            elapsedTime = new Date().getTime() - startTime // tempo que passou durante o swipe
            console.log("touchend: " + touchend +"touchendY: " + touchendY + " dist: " + dist + " elapsedTime: "+elapsedTime);           
            
            if(distY<threshold){

            if (touchend != touchstart && elapsedTime <= allowedTime && dist >=threshold && elapsedTime >= clickTime) {
                if (touchend < touchstart) {
                    var show = getPrevBannerHomeItem(atual, listaItens);
                    activeArowBannerHome(show, listaItens, btns);
                    showBannerHomeItem(show, lista);
                    atual = show;
                   
                } else{
                    var show = getNextBannerHomeItem(atual, listaItens);
                    activeArowBannerHome(show, listaItens, btns);
                    showBannerHomeItem(show, lista);
                    atual = show;
                   
                }
            } 
            else if((touchstart == touchend || (dist < 10 && distY < 10)) && elapsedTime <= clickTime) {
                var a = $(e.target).closest("a");

                if (a.hasClass("left")) {
                    var show = getPrevBannerHomeItem(atual, listaItens);

                    activeArowBannerHome(show, listaItens, btns);
                    showBannerHomeItem(show, lista);
                    atual = show;
                }

                else if (a.hasClass("right")) {
                    var show = getNextBannerHomeItem(atual, listaItens);

                    activeArowBannerHome(show, listaItens, btns);
                    showBannerHomeItem(show, lista);
                    atual = show;
                }
                else if (!a.hasClass("btn")) {
                    window.location.href = $(e.target).closest("a").attr("href");
                }
                else if (!a.hasClass("link-desabilitado")) {
                    link.href = '';
                    link.setAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
                } else {
                    var show = getPrevBannerHomeItem(atual, listaItens);

                    activeArowBannerHome(show, listaItens, btns);
                    showBannerHomeItem(show, lista);
                    atual = show;
                }
            }
        }else{
            e.preventDefault();
        }
                
        }, false);

        if (listaItens.length > 1) {
            auto();
            banner.hover(
                function () {
                    clearInterval(t)
                }, function () {
                    auto();
                })
        }

    })
</script>

<script>
</script><script>
    function slideNext(e) {
      var el = $(e.target).closest(".atomoCarrossel");
      var slideRange = el.find(".atomoCarrosselListaBox").width();
      var max  = ( el.find(".atomoCarrosselBox").length * ( el.find(".atomoCarrosselBox").width()+30)) - slideRange;
      var nextPosition = (parseInt( el.find(".atomoCarrosselListaBoxSlide").attr("data-position")) + slideRange >  max ) ? max : parseInt(el.find(".atomoCarrosselListaBoxSlide").attr("data-position")) + slideRange;
      el.find(".atomoCarrosselListaBoxSlide").attr("data-position", nextPosition);
      el.find(".atomoCarrosselListaBoxSlide").css("transform","translate3d( -"+nextPosition+"px, 0, 0)");
    }

    function slidePrev(e) {
      var el = $(e.target).closest(".atomoCarrossel");

      var slideRange = el.find(".atomoCarrosselListaBox").width();
      var prevPosition = (parseInt(el.find(".atomoCarrosselListaBoxSlide").attr("data-position")) - slideRange <  0 ) ? 0 : parseInt(el.find(".atomoCarrosselListaBoxSlide").attr("data-position")) - slideRange;
      el.find(".atomoCarrosselListaBoxSlide").attr("data-position", prevPosition);
      el.find(".atomoCarrosselListaBoxSlide").css("transform","translate3d( -"+prevPosition+"px, 0, 0)");
    }

    $(document).ready(function(){
      $(".atomoCarrossel").each(function(i){
        _this = $(this);
        //if(_this.hasClass("noCarrossel")){ return false; }
        if((_this.find(".atomoCarrosselBox").length * (_this.find(".atomoCarrosselBox").width()+30)) > _this.find(".atomoCarrosselListaBox").width()){
          _this.find(".atomoCarrosselListaBoxSlide").attr("data-position",0);
          _this.find(".navButtom.next").click(function(e){
            e.preventDefault();
            slideNext(e);
          });

          _this.find(".navButtom.prev").click(function(e){
            e.preventDefault();

            slidePrev(e);
          });

          _this.find(".atomoCarrosselListaBoxSlide")[0].addEventListener('touchstart', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            var touch = e.touches[0];
            touchstart = touch.pageX;
          }, false);

          _this.find(".atomoCarrosselListaBoxSlide")[0].addEventListener('touchmove', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            var touch = e.touches[0];
            touchend = touch.pageX
          }, false);

          _this.find(".atomoCarrosselListaBoxSlide")[0].addEventListener('touchend', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            if(touchend < touchstart){
              slideNext(e);
            }else if(touchend > touchstart){
              slidePrev(e);
            }
          }, false);
        }
      })
    })

</script><script>


    $(function(){
      $(".atomoKVTitulo").each(function(){
        preSmileEm = $(this).find(".atomoKVSmile em").eq(0);
        posSmileEm = $(this).find(".atomoKVSmile em").eq(1);



        var preSmile = preSmileEm.text().trim().split(" ");
        var posSmile = posSmileEm.text().trim().split(" ");


        if(preSmile.length > 1){
          var preSmilesFim = preSmile.splice((preSmile.length -1) , 1);
          var style = preSmileEm.attr("class");

          $(this).prepend("<span class='"+style+"'>"+preSmile.join(" ")+"</span>");
          preSmileEm.html(preSmilesFim);
        }
        if(posSmile.length > 1){
          var style = posSmileEm.find("strong").attr("class");

          var posSmilesFim = posSmile.splice(0 , 1);
          $(this).append("<strong class='"+style+"'>"+posSmile.join(" ")+"</strong>");
          posSmileEm.html("<strong class='"+style+"'>"+posSmilesFim.join(" ")+"</strong>");
        }
        $(".atomoKVTitulo").animate({opacity: 1});

if($(this).find(".atomoKVSmile em").eq(0).text() == "Smiles"){
      $(this).find(".atomoKVSmile em").eq(0).wrap("<strong></strong>");
	  $(this).find(".atomoKVSmile em").eq(1).html($(this).find(".atomoKVSmile em").eq(1).html().replace(/strong/g, "span"));

      text = $(this).find(">strong").text();
      classes = $(this).find(">strong").attr("class");
	  $(this).find(">strong").remove();
      $(this).append("<span class='"+classes+"'>"+text+"</span>");
  }

      })
    })
$('img.svg').each(function(){
	var $img = $(this);
	var imgID = $img.attr('id');
	var imgClass = $img.attr('class');
	var imgURL = $img.attr('src');

	$.get(imgURL, function(data) {
		var $svg = $(data).find('svg');
		if (typeof imgID !== 'undefined') {
			$svg = $svg.attr('id', imgID);
		}
		if (typeof imgClass !== 'undefined') {
			$svg = $svg.attr('class', imgClass+' replaced-svg');
		}
		$svg = $svg.removeAttr('xmlns:a');
		$img.replaceWith($svg);
	});
});
if($(".timer").length){
      $(".timer").each(function(){
        var _this = $(this);
        setInterval(function() {
          var h = _this.find(".hour").val();
          var m = _this.find(".minute").val();
          var i = _this.find(".second").val();

          _this.find(".cd").html(h+":"+m+":"+i);
        }, 1000);
      })
    }

		if (window.location.pathname.endsWith('minha-conta/solicitar-milhas')) {
			$('.smiles-content > .mb').css('margin-top','-20px')
		}


		(function(url){
			var flightResultUrl = '/emissao-com-milhas';

			if(url !== flightResultUrl){
				return;
			}

			$('.smiles-header').removeClass('fixed');
			$('.smiles-content').removeClass('main');

		})(window.location.pathname);
	});
</script>

		function loadScripts(url) {
			var script;
			script = document.createElement('script');

			script.src = url;
			document.body.appendChild(script);
		}

			j=d.createElement(s),dl=l!='dataLayer'?'&l='+l:'';j.async=true;j.src=
			'//www.googletagmanager.com/gtm.js?id='+i+dl;f.parentNode.insertBefore(j,f);
			})(window,document,'script','dataLayer','GTM-MDJS8L');
		}

var unknowMsg = '\u004f\u0063\u006f\u0072\u0072\u0065\u0075\u0020\u0075\u006d\u0020\u0065\u0072\u0072\u006f\u0020\u0064\u0075\u0072\u0061\u006e\u0074\u0065\u0020\u006f\u0020\u0070\u0072\u006f\u0063\u0065\u0073\u0073\u006f\u0020\u0065\u0020\u0073\u0075\u0061\u0020\u0073\u006f\u006c\u0069\u0063\u0069\u0074\u0061\u0026\u0063\u0063\u0065\u0064\u0069\u006c\u003b\u0026\u0061\u0074\u0069\u006c\u0064\u0065\u003b\u006f\u0020\u006e\u0026\u0061\u0074\u0069\u006c\u0064\u0065\u003b\u006f\u0020\u0066\u006f\u0069\u0020\u0063\u006f\u006e\u0063\u006c\u0075\u0026\u0069\u0061\u0063\u0075\u0074\u0065\u003b\u0064\u0061\u002e\u0020\u0050\u006f\u0072\u0020\u0066\u0061\u0076\u006f\u0072\u002c\u0020\u0074\u0065\u006e\u0074\u0065\u0020\u006e\u006f\u0076\u0061\u006d\u0065\u006e\u0074\u0065\u002e';

$(function(){
	var cpfServerName = '\u0043\u0050\u0046';
	var $inputBirthdayDay = $("#inputBirthdayDay");
	var $inputBirthdayMonth = $("#inputBirthdayMonth");
	var $inputBirthdayYear = $("#inputBirthdayYear");
	var $inputDocumentType = $("#inputDocumentType");
	var $inputCpf = $("#inputCpf");
	var $retrieveMemberNumberValidationBlock = $("#retrieveMemberNumberValidationBlock");
	var $retrieveMemberNumberValidationBlockMsg = $("#retrieveMemberNumberValidationBlockMsg");

	$inputBirthdayDay.mask("99");
	$inputBirthdayMonth.mask("99");
	$inputBirthdayYear.mask("9999");

	function markInvalidDate(isDateInvalid, changeOnlyCss) {
		if (isDateInvalid) {
			$inputBirthdayDay.css('border-color', 'red');
			$inputBirthdayMonth.css('border-color', 'red');
			$inputBirthdayYear.css('border-color', 'red');
			if (!changeOnlyCss) {
				$retrieveMemberNumberValidationBlockMsg.html(Liferay.Language.get('error.full.message.invalid.date'));
				$retrieveMemberNumberValidationBlock.removeClass('hide');
			}
		} else {
			$inputBirthdayDay.css('border-color', '');
			$inputBirthdayMonth.css('border-color', '');
			$inputBirthdayYear.css('border-color', '');
			if (!changeOnlyCss) {
				$retrieveMemberNumberValidationBlock.addClass('hide');
				$retrieveMemberNumberValidationBlockMsg.html('');
			}
		}
	}

	function markInvalidDocument(isDocumentInvalid, message) {
		if (isDocumentInvalid) {
			$inputDocumentType.css('border-color', 'red');
			$inputCpf.css('border-color', 'red');
			$('#dk_container_inputDocumentType').find('a.dk_toggle').css('border-color', 'red');
			if (message != '') {
				$retrieveMemberNumberValidationBlockMsg.html(message);
				$retrieveMemberNumberValidationBlock.removeClass('hide');
			}
		} else {
			$inputCpf.css('border-color', '');
			$inputDocumentType.css('border-color', '');
			$('#dk_container_inputDocumentType').find('a.dk_toggle ').css('border-color', '');
			$retrieveMemberNumberValidationBlock.addClass('hide');
			$retrieveMemberNumberValidationBlockMsg.html('');
		}
	}

	$('.selectDocument').dropkick({
		change : function(value, label) {
			$('#retrieveMemberNumberValidationBlock.tableMsg').addClass('hide');
			$("input").each(function(){
				$(this).css('border-color', '');
				$(this).val('');
		   });
			if (value.toUpperCase() === cpfServerName || value === "") {
				$inputCpf.siblings("label").html(Liferay.Language.get('label.document.number'));
				$inputCpf.mask("999.999.999-99");
				$inputCpf.attr("maxlength", "14");
				$('#inputCpf').attr('type', 'tel');
				$('#inputCpf').attr('placeholder', decodeHtml(Liferay.Language.get('label.document.number')));	 
			} else {
				$inputCpf.siblings("label").html(Liferay.Language.get('label.mail'));
				$inputCpf.unmask();
				$inputCpf.attr("maxlength", "200");
				$('#inputCpf').attr('type', 'text');
				$('#inputCpf').attr('placeholder', Liferay.Language.get('label.email'))
			}
			
			$inputCpf.val('');
		}
	});


	$.fn.validarFrmRetrieveNumber = function() {
		var valid = true;
		
		markInvalidDate(false, false);
		markInvalidDocument(false, '');
		
		if ($inputDocumentType.val() != '') {
			if ($inputDocumentType.val() === cpfServerName) {
				if (!$.fn.validateCPF.call(this, $inputCpf.val())) {
					markInvalidDocument(true, Liferay.Language.get('error.message.cpf.invalid'));
					valid = false;
				}
			} else {
				if (!validacaoEmail($inputCpf.val())) {
					markInvalidDocument(true, Liferay.Language.get('error.message.inform.valid.email'));
					valid = false;
				}
			}
		} else {
			markInvalidDocument(true, '');
		}
		
		if ($inputDocumentType.attr("readonly") != 'readonly') {
			if ($inputDocumentType.val() == '') {
				$inputDocumentType.css('border-color', 'red');
				$('#dk_container_inputDocumentType').find('a.dk_toggle ').css('border-color', 'red');
				valid = false;
			} else {
				$inputDocumentType.css('border-color', '');
				$('#dk_container_inputDocumentType').find('a.dk_toggle ').css('border-color', '');
			}
		}

		if ($inputBirthdayDay.attr("readonly") != 'readonly') {
			if ($inputBirthdayDay.val() == '') {
				markInvalidDate(true, true);
				valid = false;
			} else {
				markInvalidDate(false, true);
			}
		}

		if ($inputBirthdayMonth.attr("readonly") != 'readonly') {
			if ($inputBirthdayMonth.val() == '') {
				markInvalidDate(true, true);
				valid = false;
			} else {
				markInvalidDate(false, true);
			}
		}

		if ($inputBirthdayYear.attr("readonly") != 'readonly') {
			if ($inputBirthdayYear.val() == '') {
				markInvalidDate(true, true);
				valid = false;
			} else {
				markInvalidDate(false, true);
			}
		}

		return valid;
	};

	try {
		scriptGlobal();
	} catch (e) {
		console.error(e);
	}

});

function decodeHtml(html) {
	var txt = document.createElement("textarea");
	txt.innerHTML = html;
	return txt.value;
}	

var _headerContent = '<div class="titleModal"><img src="/smiles-default-theme/img/imgTopLoginWhite.png" alt="" class="icon"> <span>\u0052\u0065\u0063\u0075\u0070\u0065\u0072\u0061\u0072\u0020\u004e\u0026\u0075\u0061\u0063\u0075\u0074\u0065\u003b\u006d\u0065\u0072\u006f\u0020\u0053\u006d\u0069\u006c\u0065\u0073</span></div>';

AUI().use(
	'aui-modal',
	'aui-io-request',
	'node',
	'submitForm',
function(A) {
	
    var applyLinkForgetSmilesNumber = setInterval(function() {
		A.all('.showModalForgetSmilesMemberNumber').each(
			function (node) {
				clearInterval(applyLinkForgetSmilesNumber);
				node.on('click', function() {
					$("input").each(function(){
						$(this).css('border-color', '');
						$(this).val('');
				   });
					$('#retrieveMemberNumberValidationBlock.tableMsg').addClass('hide');
					$('.selectDocument').dropkick('reset');		
					$('#inputCpf').attr('type', 'tel');
					$('#inputCpf').attr("maxlength", "14");
					$('#inputCpf').mask("999.999.999-99");
					$('#inputCpf').attr('placeholder', decodeHtml(Liferay.Language.get('label.document.number')));
				    if (modalSmilesLogin) {
						modalSmilesLogin.hide();																						
					}				
				    modalRetrieveMemberNumber.show();
				});
			}
		);
	}, 500);

    var modalRetrieveMemberNumber = new A.Modal({
        bodyContent: A.one('#dialog_retrieveMemberNumber'),
        centered: true,
        headerContent: _headerContent,
        modal: true,
        draggable: false,
        render: '#modalMemberNumberForget',
        zIndex: 1100,
        width: 390,
        resizable: false,
        visible: false,
    }).render();

    var openNumber = A.one('#openNumber');
    if (openNumber) {
        openNumber.on('click', function() {
            modalRetrieveMemberNumber.show();
        });
    }
	
    A.one('body').delegate('click',
        function() {
            window.location = '/cadastro';
        },
        '#retrieveNumberSmilesRegister');

		Liferay.provide(
		        window,
		        'openModalForgetNumber',
		        function() {
		        	if (modalSmilesLogin) {
				        modalSmilesLogin.hide();
				    }
		            modalRetrieveMemberNumber.show(); 
		});

    function callBackAjax(data) {
        A.one('#btnSubmitRetrieveMemberNumber').attr('disabled', '');
        $('#forgotMemberNumberLoading').addClass('hide');
        
        var jsonReturn = '';
        
        try {
        	jsonReturn = JSON.parse(data);
        }
		catch(jsonErr) {
			jsonReturn = data;
		}

		if (jsonReturn.errorCode == 400) {
			$("#retrieveMemberNumberValidationBlock").removeClass('hide');
			$("#retrieveMemberNumberValidationBlockMsg").html(jsonReturn.errorMessage);
		}
		else {
			submitForm();
		}
    }

    A.one('#btnSubmitRetrieveMemberNumber').on(
        'click',
        function(event) {
            event.preventDefault();

            A.one('.tableMsg').setStyle('display', 'none');

            
            if ($(this).validarFrmRetrieveNumber()) {
            
    	        A.one('#btnSubmitRetrieveMemberNumber').attr('disabled', 'disabled');
	        	$('#forgotMemberNumberLoading').removeClass('hide');
			
            	var resourceURL = "https://www.smiles.com.br/home?p_p_id=smilesforgetmembernumberportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet&p_p_lifecycle=2&p_p_state=normal&p_p_mode=view&p_p_resource_id=validateFormData&p_p_cacheability=cacheLevelPage";
        		var jsNamespace = '_smilesforgetmembernumberportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_';
        		var ajaxForgetMemberNumberCall = new AjaxSmiles(resourceURL, jsNamespace);
        		ajaxForgetMemberNumberCall.addParameters("year",$("#inputBirthdayYear").val());
        		ajaxForgetMemberNumberCall.addParameters("month",$("#inputBirthdayMonth").val());
        		ajaxForgetMemberNumberCall.addParameters("day",$("#inputBirthdayDay").val());
        		if(!$.isEmptyObject(ajaxForgetMemberNumberCall.parameterList)) {
        			ajaxForgetMemberNumberCall.disableLoadingModal();
        			ajaxForgetMemberNumberCall.executeAjaxCall(null, null, callBackAjax);	
        		}
            }
        }
    );



    function submitForm() {

		A.one('#btnSubmitRetrieveMemberNumber').attr('disabled', 'disabled');
		$('#forgotMemberNumberLoading').removeClass('hide');

        A.io.request('https://www.smiles.com.br/home?p_p_id=smilesforgetmembernumberportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet&p_p_lifecycle=0&p_p_state=normal&p_p_mode=view&_smilesforgetmembernumberportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_action=retrieveMemberNumber', {
            form: {
                id: 'frmRetrieveMemberNumber'
            },
            on: {
                success: function() {

                    modalRetrieveMemberNumber.hide();
                    
                    
	                    var result = A.Node.create(this.get('responseData')).one('.modalMemberNumber');
	
	                    var modalReturn = new A.Modal({
	                        bodyContent: result,
	                        centered: true,
	                        headerContent: _headerContent,
	                        modal: true,
	                        draggable: false,
	                        render: '#modalMemberNumberForget',
	                        zIndex: 1100,
	                        width: 450,
	                        resizable: false,
	                        visible: false,
	                    }).render();
	
	                    modalReturn.show();
                },
                failure: function() {
                    $("#retrieveMemberNumberValidationBlock").removeClass('hide');
                    $("#retrieveMemberNumberValidationBlockMsg").html(unknowMsg);
                },
                complete: function() {
                    A.one('#btnSubmitRetrieveMemberNumber').attr('disabled', '');
                    
                    $('#forgotMemberNumberLoading').addClass('hide');
                }
            }
        });
    }
});

			kaleoURL.setParameter('availablePropertyModels', config.availablePropertyModels);
			kaleoURL.setParameter('ddmStructureId', config.ddmStructureId);
			kaleoURL.setParameter('draftVersion', config.draftVersion);

			kaleoURL.setParameter('name', config.name);
			kaleoURL.setParameter('openerWindowName', config.openerWindowName);
			kaleoURL.setParameter('portletResourceNamespace', config.portletResourceNamespace);
			kaleoURL.setParameter('propertiesSaveCallback', config.propertiesSaveCallback);
			kaleoURL.setParameter('refreshOpenerOnClose', config.refreshOpenerOnClose);
			kaleoURL.setParameter('saveCallback', config.saveCallback);
			kaleoURL.setParameter('version', config.version);
			kaleoURL.setWindowState('pop_up');

			config.uri = kaleoURL.toString();

			var dialogConfig = config.dialog;

			if (!dialogConfig) {
				var region = A.one(Liferay.Util.getOpener()).get('region');

				dialogConfig = {
					destroyOnHide: true
				};

				config.dialog = dialogConfig;
			}

			if (!('align' in dialogConfig)) {
				dialogConfig.align = Liferay.Util.Window.ALIGN_CENTER;
			}

			var dialogIframeConfig = config.dialogIframe;

			if (!dialogIframeConfig) {
				dialogIframeConfig = {
					closeOnEscape: false
				};

				config.dialogIframe = dialogIframeConfig;
			}

			Liferay.Util.openWindow(config);
		},
		['liferay-portlet-url']

		function doCallSelfServiceCallback(data) {
			if ( data != null && data != 'undefined') {
				var obj;
				try {
					obj = jQuery.parseJSON(data);
				} catch (e){
					obj = data; 
				}

				if ( obj.returnCode == 200) {				
					window.InbentaEvents.openEmiliaBot( obj.token );
					window.sessionStorage.setItem("chatSmiles", true);
				}			
			}
		}
	
		function doCallSelfService() {
			var namespace = '_serviceportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_';
			var url = 'https://www.smiles.com.br/home?p_p_id=serviceportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet&p_p_lifecycle=2&p_p_state=normal&p_p_mode=view&p_p_resource_id=retrieveSelfServiceURL&p_p_cacheability=cacheLevelPage';
	 			
			var callable = new AjaxSmiles(url, namespace, 'POST');
			callable.displayLoading = false;
			callable.timeout= 30000;
			callable.executeAjaxCall(function (data) {
				doCallSelfServiceCallback(data);
			});
		}

			window.InbentaEvents.openEmiliaBot('');
			window.sessionStorage.setItem("chatSmiles", true);
		} */

var _showFirstAccess = function() {
	try {
		if (modalSmilesLogin) {
			modalSmilesLogin.hide();
		}

		$('#_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_inputNumberSmiles').val('');
		$('#_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_inputCpfTwo').val('');
		modalIdentificationFirstAccess.show();
	} catch (e) {
 		console.error(e);
	}
};

A.all('#showModalFirstAccess').on('click', function(issue) {
	_showFirstAccess();			
});

Liferay.on('showFirstAccess', function(event) {
	_showFirstAccess();	
});

$('#btnSubmitIdentificationFirstAccess').on('click', function() {
	
	if (!valideFormIdentificationFirstAccess('_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_')) {
		return;
	}
	
	$('#btnSubmitResetPassword').attr('disabled', true);
	$('#firstAccessLoading').removeClass('hide');
	
	A.io.request('https://www.smiles.com.br/home?p_p_id=smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet&p_p_lifecycle=2&p_p_state=normal&p_p_mode=view&p_p_resource_id=validateMemberNumber&p_p_cacheability=cacheLevelPage', {
	 	method: 'POST',
 	 	form: { id: '_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_frmFirstAccess' },
 	 	dataType: 'json',
 	 	contentType: 'application/json',
      	on: {
			success: function() {
             	var data = this.get('responseData');
             	if ( data.errorCode == 200) {
					if(data.memberEmail.includes('@')){
						smiles.portal.PasswordReset.controller.email = data.memberEmail;
					} else { 
						smiles.portal.PasswordReset.controller.email = 'xxxxx@'+data.memberEmail;
					}		
					A.one('#send-pass-email').html(smiles.portal.PasswordReset.controller.email);			
					modalIdentificationFirstAccess.hide();
					
					memberNumber = data.memberNumber;
					smiles.portal.PasswordReset.controller.id = memberNumber;
					smiles.portal.PasswordReset.controller.cpf = data.memberCPF;
					fingerPrintDomain = data.fingerPrintDomain;
					redirectFirstAccess = data.redirectFirstAccess;
					citizenship = data.citizenship;
					
					if(data.citizenship != 'BRAZILIAN'){
						$("#cpfhide").hide();
						$("#dddhide").hide();
					}else{
						$("#cpfhide").show();
						$("#dddhide").show();
					}
					
					if (redirectFirstAccess == 'true') {
						isReset = false;
						
						modalRequestNewEmail.show();
						$('#_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_newEmail').val('');
						$('#_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_newEmailConfirmation').val('');
					} else {
						modalHowReceiveEmail.show();
					}
					
				} else if (data.errorCode == 9999999) {
					
					memberNumber = data.memberNumber;
					fingerPrintDomain = data.fingerPrintDomain;
					redirectFirstAccess = data.redirectFirstAccess;
					
					modalRequestNewEmail.show();
					$('#_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_newEmail').val('');
					$('#_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_newEmailConfirmation').val('');
				} else if (data.errorMessage == 'email.member.not.found') {
					modalIdentificationFirstAccess.hide();
					_modalFormRegister.show();
				} else if (data.errorMessage == 'cpf.not.found') {
					modalIdentificationFirstAccess.hide();
					_modalFormRegister.show();
				} else {
					$('#tblFirstAccessMessages').removeClass('hide');
					$('#pFAMessages').html(data.errorMessage);
				}
			},
			complete: function() {
				$('#btnSubmitResetPassword').attr('disabled', false);
				$('#firstAccessLoading').addClass('hide');
			}
       	}
	});
});

A.one('#btnSubmitSendEmail').on('click', function(event) {

	$('#btnSubmitSendEmail').attr('disabled', true);
	$('#forgotPasswordHowReceivePasswordLoading').removeClass('hide');

	const memberData = {};
	memberData['_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_'+'memberNumber']= smiles.portal.PasswordReset.controller.id;
	memberData['_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_'+'memberEmail'] = smiles.portal.PasswordReset.controller.email;

	A.io.request('https://www.smiles.com.br/home?p_p_id=smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet&p_p_lifecycle=2&p_p_state=normal&p_p_mode=view&p_p_resource_id=sendEmail&p_p_cacheability=cacheLevelPage', {
		method: 'POST',
		dataType: 'json',
		data: memberData,
		contentType: 'application/json', on: {
			success: function() {
				var data = this.get('responseData');

				if (data.errorCode == 200) {
					var data = this.get('responseData');
					if(smiles.portal.PasswordReset.controller.$mailModal) {
						smiles.portal.PasswordReset.controller.$mailModal.hide();
					}
					if (modalHowReceiveEmail){
						modalHowReceiveEmail.hide();
					}
					modalEmailSent.show();
					$infoReader.run(smiles.portal.PasswordReset.controller.id, null, 'SMILES');
					$('#spanEmailRestorePasswordEmailSentTo').html(smiles.portal.PasswordReset.controller.email);
				} else {
					$('#divErrorForgotPasswordEmailSent').removeClass('hide');
					A.one('#pMessagesEmailSent').html('\u0045\u0072\u0072\u006f\u0020\u0061\u006f\u0020\u0065\u006e\u0076\u0069\u0061\u0072\u0020\u0065\u006d\u0061\u0069\u006c\u002e\u0020\u0050\u006f\u0072\u0020\u0066\u0061\u0076\u006f\u0072\u002c\u0020\u0074\u0065\u006e\u0074\u0065\u0020\u006e\u006f\u0076\u0061\u006d\u0065\u006e\u0074\u0065\u0020\u006d\u0061\u0069\u0073\u0020\u0074\u0061\u0072\u0064\u0065\u002e');
				}

				callBigData(memberNumber, fingerPrintDomain);
			},
			complete: function() {
				$('#btnSubmitSendEmail').attr('disabled', false);
				$('#forgotPasswordHowReceivePasswordLoading').addClass('hide');
			}
		}
	});
});

A.one('#btnFrmSendToNewEmail').on('click', function(event) {
	if(smiles.portal.PasswordReset.controller.$mailModal) {
		smiles.portal.PasswordReset.controller.$mailModal.hide();
	}
	if (modalHowReceiveEmail){
		modalHowReceiveEmail.hide();
	}
	callBigData(smiles.portal.PasswordReset.controller.id, fingerPrintDomain);

	modalRequestNewEmail.show();
	$('#_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_newEmail').val('');
	$('#_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_newEmailConfirmation').val('');
	A.one('#formRegisterNewEmail').setStyle('display', 'block');
});

A.one('#btnConfirmNewEmail').on('click', function(event) {
	$('#btnConfirmNewEmail').attr('disabled', true);
	$('#forgotPasswordRequestNewEmailLoading').removeClass('hide');
	$('#tblsMessagesFormsRequestNewEmailRestore').addClass('hide');

	if (!valideNewEmail('_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_')) {
		return;
	}

	const memberData = {};
	memberData['_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_'+'memberNumber']= smiles.portal.PasswordReset.controller.id;
	memberData['_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_'+'memberEmail'] = smiles.portal.PasswordReset.controller.email;
	memberData['_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_'+'memberCpf']   = smiles.portal.PasswordReset.controller.cpf;

	A.io.request('https://www.smiles.com.br/home?p_p_id=smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet&p_p_lifecycle=2&p_p_state=normal&p_p_mode=view&p_p_resource_id=questionOne&p_p_cacheability=cacheLevelPage', {
	 	method: 'POST',
		dataType: 'json',
		data: memberData,
 	 	contentType: 'application/json',
 	 	form: { id: '_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_frmConfirmNewEmail' },
         	on: {
			success: function() {
				var data = this.get('responseData');

               	if (data.errorCode == 200) {

					modalRequestNewEmail.hide();
					$('.close').click().click();
					smiles.portal.PasswordReset.controller.firstAccessModal().hide();

					var responseQuestions = jQuery.parseJSON(data.bigDataQuestionResponse);

					if (responseQuestions.questions.length > 0) {
						modalQuestionnaire.show();
						buildQuestions('_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_', responseQuestions);
					} else {
						_modalFormRegister.show();
					}
				} else {
					modalRequestNewEmail.hide();
					$('.close').click().click();
					smiles.portal.PasswordReset.controller.firstAccessModal().hide();
					_modalFormRegister.show();
				}
			},
			complete: function() {

				$('#btnConfirmNewEmail').attr('disabled', false);
				$('#forgotPasswordRequestNewEmailLoading').addClass('hide');
				$("#_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_cpfs").val(smiles.portal.PasswordReset.controller.cpf).attr('readonly', 'readonly');
                $("#_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_smilesNumbers").val(smiles.portal.PasswordReset.controller.id).attr('readonly', 'readonly');
				$('#_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_emailsConfirmation').val($('#_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_newEmailConfirmation').val()).attr('readonly', 'readonly');
				$('#_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_emails').val($('#_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_newEmailConfirmation').val()).attr('readonly', 'readonly');
			}
          	}
	});
});

A.one('#btnSubmitQuestionnaire').on('click', function(event) {
	if(questionTimers!=null){
	  for(var i=0; i< questionTimers.length; i++) {
	     if (questionTimers[i].Timer !== undefined && questionTimers[i].Timer !== null) {
	   		questionTimers[i].stopTimer();
	   	 }
	  }
	 }
	var answers = '';    	
	A.all('#frmQuestionnaire select').each(function(node) {
		node.setStyle('border-color', '');
		answers = answers + ';' + node.get('value');
	});
	
	A.one('#questionOne').set('value', answers);
	
	modalQuestionnaire.hide();
	var modal = new SmilesWaitingModalManager('grp_loading_modal');
	modal.showModal();
	A.io.request('https://www.smiles.com.br/home?p_p_id=smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet&p_p_lifecycle=2&p_p_state=normal&p_p_mode=view&p_p_resource_id=sendAnswers&p_p_cacheability=cacheLevelPage', {
	 	method: 'POST',
 	 	dataType: 'json',
 	 	contentType: 'application/json',
 	 	form: { id: 'frmQuestionnaire' },
         	on: {
				success: function() {
					var data = this.get('responseData');
	                	modal.closeModal();
	                	if (data.errorCode == 200) {
	                		$infoReader.run(data.memberNumber, null, 'SMILES');
	                		modalOtherEmailSent.show();
					} else {
						_modalFormRegister.show();				
					}
				},
				complete: function() {
               		limparFormulario('_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_');
				}
          	}
	});
});

A.one('#btEnterTwo').on('click', function() {
	
	$('#btEnterTwo').attr('disabled', true);
	
	if (valideForm('_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_')) {
	
		_modalFormRegister.hide();
		var loading = new SmilesWaitingModalManager('grp_loading_modal');
		loading.showModal();
		A.io.request('https://www.smiles.com.br/home?p_p_id=smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet&p_p_lifecycle=2&p_p_state=normal&p_p_mode=view&p_p_resource_id=sendAnswers2&p_p_cacheability=cacheLevelPage', {
	 	method: 'POST',
 	 	dataType: 'json',
 	 	form: { id: '_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_frmsSendAnswers2' },
 	 	contentType: 'application/json',
         	on: {
				success: function() {
					var data = this.get('responseData');
					loading.closeModal();
					
					var modalTitle = Liferay.Language.get("label.attention");
					var modal = new SmilesErrorModalManager('grp_error_modal', null, modalTitle);
					modal.addErrorMessage('resetpassword', data.errorMessage);
					modal.showModal();
				},
				complete: function() {
                    $('#btEnterTwo').removeAttr('disabled');
                    limparFormulario('_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_');
                }
          	}
		});
	} else {
		$('#btEnterTwo').removeAttr('disabled');
		$('#_smilesmemberpasswordresetportlet_WAR_smilesaccountmanagerportlet_firstNames').focus();
	}
});
